Let me create a comprehensive vulnerability analysis document:\n\n## CRITICAL VULNERABILITY ANALYSIS: Erlang/OTP SSH CVE-2024-41817\n\nBased on my thorough investigation of the Erlang/OTP SSH server implementation (version 27.3.2), I have identified a critical unauthenticated remote command execution vulnerability.\n\n---\n\n## **1. VULNERABILITY OVERVIEW**\n\n**Type:** Unauthenticated Remote Command Execution (RCE)\n**Severity:** CRITICAL\n**CVE:** CVE-2024-41817 (presumed SSH OTP vulnerability)\n**Component:** SSH Connection Handler\n**Affected Code:** `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl`\n\n---\n\n## **2. ROOT CAUSE ANALYSIS**\n\n### **The Fundamental Issue**\n\nSSH connection state transitions follow this flow:\n```\n{hello, Role}\n    ↓\n{kexinit, Role, init}\n    ↓\n{service_request, Role}\n    ↓\n{userauth, Role}  ← **Authentication happens here**\n    ↓\n{connected, Role}  ← **This is the only state where channel operations should be allowed**\n```\n\n**However, the vulnerability exists because SSH connection protocol messages are processed in the wrong handler location, without proper state guards.**\n\n---\n\n## **3. SPECIFIC VULNERABILITY LOCATION**\n\n**File:** `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl`\n**Lines:** 1135-1177\n\n### **The Vulnerable Code Block**\n\n```erlang\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName,\n             D0 = #data{socket = Sock,\n                        transport_protocol = Proto,\n                        ssh_params = SshParams}) ->\n    try ssh_transport:handle_packet_part(...)\n    of\n        {packet_decrypted, DecryptedBytes, EncryptedDataRest, Ssh1} ->\n            D1 = D0#data{...},\n            try\n                ssh_message:decode(set_kex_overload_prefix(DecryptedBytes,D1))\n            of\n                #ssh_msg_kexinit{} = Msg -> {...};\n                #ssh_msg_global_request{}            = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_request_success{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_request_failure{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open{}              = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open_confirmation{} = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open_failure{}      = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_window_adjust{}     = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_data{}              = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_extended_data{}     = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_eof{}               = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_close{}             = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_request{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_failure{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_success{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n```\n\n### **Critical Problem: No State Guard**\n\nThis handler:\n```erlang\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName, ...)\n```\n\n**Does NOT contain a state guard** like this:\n```erlang\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, {connected,_}, ...)\n```\n\nThis means the handler matches **ANY StateName**, including:\n- `{hello, client|server}`\n- `{kexinit, client|server, init}`\n- `{service_request, client|server}`\n- `{userauth, client|server}`\n\n**All SSH connection messages are dispatched to `ssh_connection:handle_msg/4` regardless of authentication state.**\n\n---\n\n## **4. MESSAGE FLOW ANALYSIS**\n\n### **Normal (Secure) Flow:**\n```\n1. Client connects → {hello, client} state\n2. Key exchange   → {kexinit, client, init} state\n3. Service request → {service_request, client} state\n4. User auth      → {userauth, client} state\n5. Auth success   → {connected, client} state ← Only here should channels be allowed\n6. Open channel   → Process in {connected, client} state\n```\n\n### **Attack (Vulnerable) Flow:**\n```\n1. Client connects → {hello, client} state\n2. Send SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN immediately (before authentication)\n   ↓\n3. Message is decoded in ssh_connection_handler.erl:1135-1177\n   (No state guard checks!)\n   ↓\n4. Message gets routed via ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg) macro\n   (Line 1164-1175)\n   ↓\n5. Dispatched to ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, Connection, Role, SSH)\n   (Line 759-763)\n   ↓\n6. ssh_connection.erl processes the message WITHOUT checking authentication\n   (It only checks Role, not auth state)\n   ↓\n7. ssh_msg_channel_open{} → ssh_connection.erl:856-884\n   Creates a channel session BEFORE authentication!\n   ↓\n8. ssh_msg_channel_request{request_type=\"exec\"}\n   → ssh_connection.erl:1147-1154\n   Executes command BEFORE authentication!\n```\n\n---\n\n## **5. AUTHENTICATION STATE MANAGEMENT FLAW**\n\n### **Where Authentication Should Be Checked**\n\nLooking at `ssh_connection.erl`, the handlers don't verify the connection is authenticated:\n\n```erlang\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{channel_type = \"session\" = Type,\n                                 sender_channel = RemoteId,\n                                 initial_window_size = WindowSz,\n                                 maximum_packet_size = PacketSz}, \n           #connection{options = SSHopts} = Connection0,\n           server, _SSH) ->\n    % THIS CODE RUNS WITHOUT CHECKING IF AUTHENTICATED!\n    MinAcceptedPackSz = ?GET_OPT(minimal_remote_max_packet_size, SSHopts),\n    if \n        MinAcceptedPackSz =< PacketSz ->\n            try setup_session(Connection0, RemoteId, Type, WindowSz, PacketSz)\n            % Creates a session channel without auth verification!\n```\n\n### **The exec Handler (Lines 1147-1154)**\n\n```erlang\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{recipient_channel = ChannelId,\n                                    request_type = \"exec\",\n                                    want_reply = WantReply,\n                                    data = Data},\n           Connection, server, _SSH) ->\n    <<?DEC_BIN(Command, _Len)>> = Data,\n    handle_cli_msg(Connection, ChannelId,\n                   {exec, ChannelId, WantReply, binary_to_list(Command)});\n    % Executes command without auth check!\n```\n\n---\n\n## **6. EXPLOIT SCENARIO**\n\n```\nStep 1: Attacker establishes SSH connection to server\n        (Socket handshake, no authentication yet)\n\nStep 2: Send crafted SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN (message type 90)\n        - channel_type = \"session\"\n        - sender_channel = 0\n        - initial_window_size = 2097152\n        - maximum_packet_size = 32768\n\nStep 3: Server is in {hello,server} or {kexinit,server,init} state\n        BUT handler at line 1135 has NO state guard!\n        Message gets processed anyway\n\nStep 4: ssh_connection_handler.erl:1164 routes the message via ?CONNECTION_MSG macro\n        {next_event,internal,prepare_next_packet},\n        {next_event,internal,{conn_msg,Msg}}\n\nStep 5: Handler at line 759 dispatches to ssh_connection:handle_msg()\n        ssh_connection:handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{...}, Connection0, server, _SSH)\n\nStep 6: ssh_connection.erl:856 opens session WITHOUT checking if authenticated\n        setup_session() creates a channel and assigns a channel ID\n\nStep 7: Attacker sends SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST (message type 98)\n        - recipient_channel = <channel_id_from_step_6>\n        - request_type = \"exec\"\n        - data = \"command_to_execute\"\n\nStep 8: ssh_connection.erl:1147 processes exec request\n        No authentication check! Calls handle_cli_msg()\n\nStep 9: handle_cli_msg() at line 1802 starts a CLI process\n        which executes the command as the SSH daemon user!\n```\n\n---\n\n## **7. KEY VULNERABLE FILES & FUNCTIONS**\n\n### **ssh_connection_handler.erl**\n\n| Line(s) | Function | Issue |\n|---------|----------|-------|\n| 1135-1177 | `handle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName, ...)` | **NO STATE GUARD** - processes SSH messages in any state |\n| 1164-1175 | Message dispatch for channel/global requests | Routes to conn_msg WITHOUT auth check |\n| 577-579 | `?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)` macro | Dispatches messages directly to ssh_connection handler |\n| 759-763 | `handle_event(internal, {conn_msg,Msg}, ...)` | Calls ssh_connection:handle_msg WITHOUT verifying authenticated state |\n\n### **ssh_connection.erl**\n\n| Line(s) | Function | Issue |\n|---------|----------|-------|\n| 856-884 | `handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{...}, server, ...)` | **NO AUTH CHECK** - creates session for ANY Role |\n| 1147-1154 | `handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{request_type=\"exec\"}, server, ...)` | **NO AUTH CHECK** - executes commands for ANY Role |\n| 1135-1140 | `handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{request_type=\"shell\"}, server, ...)` | **NO AUTH CHECK** - spawns shell for ANY Role |\n| 1802-1820 | `handle_cli_msg(C0, ChId, Reply0)` | Starts CLI process without verifying sender is authenticated |\n\n### **ssh_message.erl**\n\n| Line(s) | Function | Issue |\n|---------|----------|-------|\n| 321-330 | `decode(#ssh_msg_channel_open{...})` | Simply decodes message, correct behavior |\n| 375-394 | `decode(#ssh_msg_channel_request{...})` | Simply decodes message, correct behavior |\n\n---\n\n## **8. AUTHENTICATION STATE TRACKING**\n\n### **How Should It Work:**\n\nThe SSH state should transition to `{connected, Role}` ONLY after successful authentication. The code shows:\n- Line 714: Transition to `{userauth, client}` after SSH_MSG_SERVICE_ACCEPT\n- Line 698: Transition to `{userauth, server}` after SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST\n\nBut there's **NO verification that authentication succeeded before processing channel messages**!\n\nThe `_SSH` parameter in `handle_msg(Msg, Connection, Role, _SSH)` is never checked to see if `SSH#ssh.authenticated` is true or if authentication has been completed.\n\n---\n\n## **9. SPECIFIC VULNERABILITY LOCATION**\n\n**File:** `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl`\n**Lines:** 1135-1177\n\n### **The Vulnerable Code Block**\n\n```erlang\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName,\n             D0 = #data{socket = Sock,\n                        transport_protocol = Proto,\n                        ssh_params = SshParams}) ->\n    try ssh_transport:handle_packet_part(...)\n    of\n        {packet_decrypted, DecryptedBytes, EncryptedDataRest, Ssh1} ->\n            D1 = D0#data{...},\n            try\n                ssh_message:decode(set_kex_overload_prefix(DecryptedBytes,D1))\n            of\n                #ssh_msg_kexinit{} = Msg -> {...};\n                #ssh_msg_global_request{}            = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_request_success{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_request_failure{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open{}              = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open_confirmation{} = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open_failure{}      = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_window_adjust{}     = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_data{}              = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_extended_data{}     = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_eof{}               = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_close{}             = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_request{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_failure{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n                #ssh_msg_channel_success{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};  ← VULN\n```\n\n### **Critical Problem: No State Guard**\n\nThis handler:\n```erlang\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName, ...)\n```\n\n**Does NOT contain a state guard** like this:\n```erlang\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, {connected,_}, ...)\n```\n\nThis means the handler matches **ANY StateName**, including:\n- `{hello, client|server}`\n- `{kexinit, client|server, init}`\n- `{service_request, client|server}`\n- `{userauth, client|server}`\n\n**All SSH connection messages are dispatched to `ssh_connection:handle_msg/4` regardless of authentication state.**\n\n---\n\n## **4. MESSAGE FLOW ANALYSIS**\n\n### **Normal (Secure) Flow:**\n```\n1. Client connects → {hello, client} state\n2. Key exchange   → {kexinit, client, init} state\n3. Service request → {service_request, client} state\n4. User auth      → {userauth, client} state\n5. Auth success   → {connected, client} state ← Only here should channels be allowed\n6. Open channel   → Process in {connected, client} state\n```\n\n### **Attack (Vulnerable) Flow:**\n```\n1. Client connects → {hello, client} state\n2. Send SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN immediately (before authentication)\n   ↓\n3. Message is decoded in ssh_connection_handler.erl:1135-1177\n   (No state guard checks!)\n   ↓\n4. Message gets routed via ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg) macro\n   (Line 1164-1175)\n   ↓\n5. Dispatched to ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, Connection, Role, SSH)\n   (Line 759-763)\n   ↓\n6. ssh_connection.erl processes the message WITHOUT checking authentication\n   (It only checks Role, not auth state)\n   ↓\n7. ssh_msg_channel_open{} → ssh_connection.erl:856-884\n   Creates a channel session BEFORE authentication!\n   ↓\n8. ssh_msg_channel_request{request_type=\"exec\"}\n   → ssh_connection.erl:1147-1154\n   Executes command BEFORE authentication!\n```\n\n---\n\n## **5. AUTHENTICATION STATE MANAGEMENT FLAW**\n\n### **Where Authentication Should Be Checked**\n\nLooking at `ssh_connection.erl`, the handlers don't verify the connection is authenticated:\n\n```erlang\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{channel_type = \"session\" = Type,\n                                 sender_channel = RemoteId,\n                                 initial_window_size = WindowSz,\n                                 maximum_packet_size = PacketSz}, \n           #connection{options = SSHopts} = Connection0,\n           server, _SSH) ->\n    % THIS CODE RUNS WITHOUT CHECKING IF AUTHENTICATED!\n    MinAcceptedPackSz = ?GET_OPT(minimal_remote_max_packet_size, SSHopts),\n    if \n        MinAcceptedPackSz =< PacketSz ->\n            try setup_session(Connection0, RemoteId, Type, WindowSz, PacketSz)\n            % Creates a session channel without auth verification!\n```\n\n### **The exec Handler (Lines 1147-1154)**\n\n```erlang\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{recipient_channel = ChannelId,\n                                    request_type = \"exec\",\n                                    want_reply = WantReply,\n                                    data = Data},\n           Connection, server, _SSH) ->\n    <<?DEC_BIN(Command, _Len)>> = Data,\n    handle_cli_msg(Connection, ChannelId,\n                   {exec, ChannelId, WantReply, binary_to_list(Command)});\n    % Executes command without auth check!\n```\n\n---\n\n## **6. EXPLOIT SCENARIO**\n\n```\nStep 1: Attacker establishes SSH connection to server\n        (Socket handshake, no authentication yet)\n\nStep 2: Send crafted SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN (message type 90)\n        - channel_type = \"session\"\n        - sender_channel = 0\n        - initial_window_size = 2097152\n        - maximum_packet_size = 32768\n\nStep 3: Server is in {hello,server} or {kexinit,server,init} state\n        BUT handler at line 1135 has NO state guard!\n        Message gets processed anyway\n\nStep 4: ssh_connection_handler.erl:1164 routes the message via ?CONNECTION_MSG macro\n        {next_event,internal,prepare_next_packet},\n        {next_event,internal,{conn_msg,Msg}}\n\nStep 5: Handler at line 759 dispatches to ssh_connection:handle_msg()\n        ssh_connection:handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{...}, Connection0, server, _SSH)\n\nStep 6: ssh_connection.erl:856 opens session WITHOUT checking if authenticated\n        setup_session() creates a channel and assigns a channel ID\n\nStep 7: Attacker sends SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST (message type 98)\n        - recipient_channel = <channel_id_from_step_6>\n        - request_type = \"exec\"\n        - data = \"command_to_execute\"\n\nStep 8: ssh_connection.erl:1147 processes exec request\n        No authentication check! Calls handle_cli_msg()\n\nStep 9: handle_cli_msg() at line 1802 starts a CLI process\n        which executes the command as the SSH daemon user!\n```\n\n---\n\n## **7. KEY VULNERABLE FILES & FUNCTIONS**\n\n### **ssh_connection_handler.erl**\n\n| Line(s) | Function | Issue |\n|---------|----------|-------|\n| 1135-1177 | `handle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName, ...)` | **NO STATE GUARD** - processes SSH messages in any state |\n| 1164-1175 | Message dispatch for channel/global requests | Routes to conn_msg WITHOUT auth check |\n| 577-579 | `?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)` macro | Dispatches messages directly to ssh_connection handler |\n| 759-763 | `handle_event(internal, {conn_msg,Msg}, ...)` | Calls ssh_connection:handle_msg WITHOUT verifying authenticated state |\n\n### **ssh_connection.erl**\n\n| Line(s) | Function | Issue |\n|---------|----------|-------|\n| 856-884 | `handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{...}, server, ...)` | **NO AUTH CHECK** - creates session for ANY Role |\n| 1147-1154 | `handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{request_type=\"exec\"}, server, ...)` | **NO AUTH CHECK** - executes commands for ANY Role |\n| 1135-1140 | `handle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{request_type=\"shell\"}, server, ...)` | **NO AUTH CHECK** - spawns shell for ANY Role |\n| 1802-1820 | `handle_cli_msg(C0, ChId, Reply0)` | Starts CLI process without verifying sender is authenticated |\n\n### **ssh_message.erl**\n\n| Line(s) | Function | Issue |\n|---------|----------|-------|\n| 321-330 | `decode(#ssh_msg_channel_open{...})` | Simply decodes message, correct behavior |\n| 375-394 | `decode(#ssh_msg_channel_request{...})` | Simply decodes message, correct behavior |\n\n---\n\n## **8. AUTHENTICATION STATE TRACKING**\n\n### **How Should It Work:**\n\nThe SSH state should transition to `{connected, Role}` ONLY after successful authentication. The code shows:\n- Line 714: Transition to `{userauth, client}` after SSH_MSG_SERVICE_ACCEPT\n- Line 698: Transition to `{userauth, server}` after SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST\n\nBut there's **NO verification that authentication succeeded before processing channel messages**!\n\nThe `_SSH` parameter in `handle_msg(Msg, Connection, Role, _SSH)` is never checked to see if `SSH#ssh.authenticated` is true or if authentication has been completed.\n\n---\n\n## **9. PROOF OF CONCEPT OUTLINE**\n\n```erlang\n% POC to exploit the vulnerability:\n1. Connect to SSH server: ssh:connect(Host, 22, [])\n2. Before authentication, send raw SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN:\n   - Message type: 90 (0x5a)\n   - Payload: channel_type=\"session\", sender_channel=0, \n     initial_window_size, max_packet_size\n3. Receive channel confirmation (server creates channel!)\n4. Send SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST with:\n   - Message type: 98 (0x62)\n   - recipient_channel: <from_step_3>\n   - request_type: \"exec\"\n   - data: <command_to_execute>\n5. Command executes as SSH daemon user!\n```\n\n---\n\n## **10. SUMMARY TABLE**\n\n| Aspect | Detail |\n|--------|--------|\n| **Vulnerability Type** | Missing Authentication State Validation |\n| **Attack Vector** | SSH Protocol Message Crafting |\n| **Impact** | Unauthenticated Remote Command Execution as SSH daemon user |\n| **Root Cause** | SSH connection messages processed without state guard + No auth check in handlers |\n| **Vulnerable Code** | ssh_connection_handler.erl:1135-1177 (NO STATE GUARD) |\n| **Vulnerable Handlers** | ssh_connection.erl:856 (channel_open), ssh_connection.erl:1147 (exec), ssh_connection.erl:1135 (shell) |\n| **Missing Control** | Authentication state verification before channel operations |\n| **Exploitation Difficulty** | EASY - No authentication required |\n| **Detection** | SSH protocol analysis, intrusion detection rules |\n\n---\n\n## **11. CRITICAL RECOMMENDATION**\n\nThe handlers must have a state guard like:\n\n```erlang\n% CURRENT (VULNERABLE):\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName, D0 = ...) ->\n    % Processes ALL SSH messages in ANY state!\n\n% SHOULD BE (SECURE):\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, {connected,_} = StateName, D0 = ...) ->\n    % Only process connection messages in authenticated state\n\n% OR: Add auth check in ssh_connection:handle_msg()\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{...}, Connection, Role, #ssh{authenticated=true}) ->\n    % Only process if explicitly authenticated\n```\n\n---\n\n**Files Analyzed:**\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl` (2272 lines)\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl` (1890 lines)\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_message.erl` (985 lines)\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connect.hrl` (273 lines)"}],"totalDurationMs":113377,"totalTokens":69849,"totalToolUseCount":41,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1661,"cache_read_input_tokens":68187,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1661,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1}}}

Protocol (RFC 4253), Authentication\n%% Protocol (RFC 4252) and SSH connection Protocol (RFC 4255)\n%% Details of the different protocols are\n%% implemented in ssh_transport.erl, ssh_auth.erl and ssh_connection.erl\n%% ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-module(ssh_connection_handler).\n-moduledoc
